Folio Assistant Core

Keywords are extracted content: they are built when the site is published, into the derived keywords graph (assets/keywords/, folio-keywords/v2), never into an entry’s ingest record. 299 entries; the methods and the papers behind them are on the Indexing evidence page.

How the variants agree

Each column is a share, averaged over entries (or sections, for headings). Headings: section keywords their own heading names. Author keywords: the authors’ own “Keywords:” or “Index Terms” line, found by some document keyword (15 entries carry one; ingestion records no PDF metadata keywords). Glossary: document keywords that are a glossary term’s label. A higher share is agreement, not correctness: a latent variant is meant to propose words the text does not use.
